
=== scripts/convert.sh ===
1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
2: #
3: # convert.sh — Convert canonical SKILL.md files into 11 tool-specific formats.
4: #
5: # Reads all skills from skills/**/SKILL.md and writes
6: # converted output to integrations/<tool>/. Run this before install.sh.
7: #
8: # Usage:
9: #   scripts/convert.sh [--tool <name>] [--out <dir>] [--parallel] [--jobs N] [--help]
10: #
11: # Tools:
12: #   claude-code  antigravity  gemini-cli  opencode  cursor  openclaw
13: #   qwen         kimi         aider       windsurf  copilot  all (default)
14: #
15: # Options:
16: #   --tool NAME      Convert for a single tool only
17: #   --out DIR        Override output directory (default: integrations/)
18: #   --parallel       Run per-tool conversions concurrently (--tool all only)
19: #   --jobs N         Parallel worker count (default: nproc / sysctl)
20: #   --help           Show this help and exit
21: #
22: # Exit codes: 0 success, 1 per-skill error, 2 argument error
23: #
24: # The 11-tool target list and the soul/agents body-split used by
25: # convert_openclaw are adapted from msitarzewski/agency-agents (MIT). See
26: # ACKNOWLEDGMENTS.md at the repo root.
27: 
28: set -euo pipefail
29: 
30: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
31: LIB_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/lib"
32: 
33: # shellcheck source=scripts/lib/term.sh
34: . "$LIB_DIR/term.sh"
35: # shellcheck source=scripts/lib/platform.sh
36: . "$LIB_DIR/platform.sh"
37: # shellcheck source=scripts/lib/slug.sh
38: . "$LIB_DIR/slug.sh"
39: # shellcheck source=scripts/lib/frontmatter.sh
40: . "$LIB_DIR/frontmatter.sh"
41: 
42: REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/.." && pwd)"
43: OUT_DIR="$REPO_ROOT/integrations"
44: SKILLS_ROOT="$REPO_ROOT/skills"
45: TODAY="$(date -u +%Y-%m-%d)"
46: 
47: ALL_TOOLS=(claude-code copilot antigravity gemini-cli opencode cursor openclaw qwen kimi aider windsurf)
48: 
49: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
50: # Usage
51: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
52: usage() {
53:   sed -n '3,22p' "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'
54:   exit 0
55: }
56: 
57: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
58: # Walk skills in deterministic ASCII order.
59: # Prints absolute paths of all SKILL.md files, sorted by category/slug.
60: # Mirrors catalog.sh's inventory filter exactly (the single source of truth for
61: # "what counts as a shippable skill"): -maxdepth 3 drops bundled node_modules /
62: # *-workspace SKILL.md scaffolds nested below a skill root, and archive/ +
63: # in-progress/ are excluded so deprecated and WIP skills never convert to hosts.
64: # Keep this in lockstep with catalog.sh:_inventory — a divergence is how the
65: # advertised convertible-skill count drifts from what actually ships.
66: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
67: collect_skills() {
68:   find "$SKILLS_ROOT" -maxdepth 3 -name "SKILL.md" -type f 2>/dev/null \
69:     | grep -v "$SKILLS_ROOT/archive/" \
70:     | grep -v "$SKILLS_ROOT/in-progress/" \
71:     | sort
72: }
73: 
74: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
75: # derive_category_slug <skill_file>
76: # Sets SKILL_CATEGORY and SKILL_SLUG globals.
77: # Special case: skills/orchestrator/SKILL.md → category=orchestrator, slug=orchestrator
78: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
79: derive_category_slug() {
80:   local file="$1"
81:   local skill_dir
82:   skill_dir="$(dirname "$file")"
83:   SKILL_SLUG="$(basename "$skill_dir")"
84:   local parent_dir
85:   parent_dir="$(basename "$(dirname "$skill_dir")")"
86:   # When parent_dir == "skills", the skill lives directly in skills/<name>/
87:   # (i.e., orchestrator). Treat category as the slug itself.
88:   if [[ "$parent_dir" == "skills" ]]; then
89:     SKILL_CATEGORY="$SKILL_SLUG"
90:   else
91:     SKILL_CATEGORY="$parent_dir"
92:   fi
93: }
94: 
95: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
96: # write_file_verbose <dest_path> <content_on_stdin>
97: # Creates parent dirs and writes content from stdin.
98: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
99: write_file_from_stdin() {
100:   local dest="$1"
101:   mkdir -p "$(dirname "$dest")"
102:   cat > "$dest"
103: }
104: 
105: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
106: # copy_references <skill_file> <dest_dir>
107: # Copies skill's references/ dir into dest_dir/references/ if it exists.
108: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
109: copy_references() {
110:   local skill_file="$1" dest_dir="$2"
111:   local refs_src
112:   refs_src="$(dirname "$skill_file")/references"
113:   if [[ -d "$refs_src" ]]; then
114:     mkdir -p "$dest_dir/references"
115:     ats_cp_r "$refs_src" "$dest_dir/references"
116:   fi
117: }
118: 
119: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
120: # copy_scripts <skill_file> <dest_dir> [prefix]
121: # Copies skill's scripts/ dir into dest_dir/scripts/ — or dest_dir/<prefix>-scripts/
122: # when a prefix is given (flat layouts) — preserving executable bits. A converted
123: # skill whose body points at a bundled script must ship it (PF1); before this,
124: # converted skills shipped dangling script references.
125: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
126: copy_scripts() {
127:   local skill_file="$1" dest_dir="$2" prefix="${3:-}"
128:   local scripts_src target
129:   scripts_src="$(dirname "$skill_file")/scripts"
130:   if [[ -d "$scripts_src" ]]; then
131:     if [[ -n "$prefix" ]]; then
132:       target="$dest_dir/${prefix}-scripts"
133:     else
134:       target="$dest_dir/scripts"
135:     fi
136:     mkdir -p "$target"
137:     ats_cp_r "$scripts_src" "$target"
138:     # Strip local build/debris dirs that have no business shipping — a skill's
139:     # scripts/ dir can accumulate npm installs and bytecode caches locally.
140:     find "$target" -type d \( -name '__pycache__' -o -name 'node_modules' \) -prune -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true
141:     find "$target" -type f -name '.DS_Store' -delete 2>/dev/null || true
142:     find "$target" -type f \( -name '*.sh' -o -name '*.py' \) -exec chmod +x {} +
143:   fi
144: }
145: 
146: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
147: # inline_references <skill_file>
148: # Print each reference file under a ## Reference: <name> header.
149: # Used by cursor, qwen, kimi. Bundled scripts/ can't be inlined into markdown —
150: # those converters ship them as a <slug>-scripts/ sibling via copy_scripts.
151: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
152: inline_references() {
153:   local skill_file="$1"
154:   local refs_src ref_file ref_name
155:   refs_src="$(dirname "$skill_file")/references"
156:   if [[ -d "$refs_src" ]]; then
157:     while IFS= read -r ref_file; do
158:       ref_name="$(basename "$ref_file" .md)"
159:       printf '\n## Reference: %s\n\n' "$ref_name"
160:       cat "$ref_file"
161:     done < <(find "$refs_src" -maxdepth 1 -type f | sort)
162:   fi
163: }
164: 
165: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
166: # Converter: claude-code
167: # Passthrough — preserve full canonical layout + references.
168: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
169: convert_claude_code() {
170:   local file="$1" category="$2" slug="$3"
171:   local dest_dir="$OUT_DIR/claude-code/$category/$slug"
172:   local dest_file="$dest_dir/SKILL.md"
173:   mkdir -p "$dest_dir"
174:   cp "$file" "$dest_file"
175:   copy_references "$file" "$dest_dir"
176:   copy_scripts "$file" "$dest_dir"
177: }
178: 
179: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
180: # convert_hooks_claude_code
181: # Emit the hooks layer for Claude Code: copy the canonical hooks/ tree into
182: # integrations/claude-code/hooks/ and generate integrations/claude-code/hooks.json
183: # in Claude Code settings-hook format. Events map to the run-with-flags wrapper
184: # under "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"/.claude/ats-hooks/ (the namespaced install target).
185: # Only Claude Code has a native lifecycle-hook system; other tools skip (see main).
186: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
187: convert_hooks_claude_code() {
188:   local hooks_src="$REPO_ROOT/hooks"
189:   if [[ ! -d "$hooks_src" ]]; then
190:     ats_warn "hooks: source tree $hooks_src not found — skipping hooks emission"
191:     return 0
192:   fi
193: 
194:   local cc_dir="$OUT_DIR/claude-code"
195:   local hooks_dest="$cc_dir/hooks"
196:   mkdir -p "$hooks_dest"
197:   ats_cp_r "$hooks_src" "$hooks_dest"
198: 
199:   # Preserve executable bits on the wrapper + scripts (ats_cp_r uses cp -r).
200:   [[ -f "$hooks_dest/run-with-flags.sh" ]] && chmod +x "$hooks_dest/run-with-flags.sh"
201:   if [[ -d "$hooks_dest/scripts" ]]; then
202:     find "$hooks_dest/scripts" -type f -name '*.sh' -exec chmod +x {} +
203:     find "$hooks_dest/scripts" -type f -name '*.py' -exec chmod +x {} +
204:   fi
205:   [[ -f "$hooks_dest/lib/hook-flags.sh" ]] && chmod +x "$hooks_dest/lib/hook-flags.sh"
206: 
207:   # Generate hooks.json (Claude Code settings-hook format) from the manifest.
208:   # Each manifest event maps to: run-with-flags.sh <id>, invoked from the
209:   # namespaced install dir. python3 stdlib only.
210:   local manifest="$hooks_src/hooks.manifest.json"
211:   python3 - "$manifest" > "$cc_dir/hooks.json" <<'PYEOF'
212: import json, sys
213: manifest = sys.argv[1]
214: with open(manifest) as f:
215:     data = json.load(f)
216: 
217: wrapper = '"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"/.claude/ats-hooks/run-with-flags.sh'
218: events = {}
219: for h in data.get("hooks", []):
220:     ev = h.get("event")
221:     hid = h.get("id")
222:     if not ev or not hid:
223:         continue
224:     entry = {
225:         "hooks": [
226:             {"type": "command", "command": '%s %s' % (wrapper, hid)}
227:         ]
228:     }
229:     events.setdefault(ev, []).append(entry)
230: 
231: # Claude Code settings.json "hooks" shape: { "<Event>": [ { hooks: [...] } ] }
232: print(json.dumps({"hooks": events}, indent=2))
233: PYEOF
234: 
235:   ats_ok "Wrote integrations/claude-code/hooks/ + hooks.json"
236: }
237: 
238: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
239: # Converter: copilot
240: # Flat layout. Strip agent-role fields. Copy references as <slug>-references/.
241: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
242: convert_copilot() {
243:   local file="$1" slug="$3"
244:   # shellcheck disable=SC2034 # category unused but kept for consistent signature
245:   local category="$2"
246:   local dest_dir="$OUT_DIR/copilot"
247:   local dest_file="$dest_dir/${slug}.md"
248:   local name version description refs_src
249:   mkdir -p "$dest_dir"
250: 
251:   name="$(get_field "name" "$file")"
252:   version="$(get_field "version" "$file")"
253:   description="$(get_field_raw "description" "$file")"
254:   local body
255:   body="$(get_body "$file")"
256: 
257:   # Write stripped frontmatter + body
258:   {
259:     printf -- '---\n'
260:     printf 'name: %s\n' "$name"
261:     printf 'version: %s\n' "$version"
262:     # description may be multiline — write as block literal
263:     printf 'description: |\n'
264:     while IFS= read -r dline; do
265:       printf '  %s\n' "$dline"
266:     done <<< "$description"
267:     printf -- '---\n'
268:     printf '%s\n' "$body"
269:   } > "$dest_file"
270: 
271:   # Emit per-skill stderr warning only when something was actually stripped
272:   # (avoids false-positive noise on minimal skills with neither field).
273:   if fm_has_field "allowed-tools" "$file" || fm_has_field "owns" "$file"; then
274:     printf '[copilot] stripped allowed-tools/owns from %s\n' "$slug" >&2
275:   fi
276: 
277:   # Copy references + scripts alongside
278:   refs_src="$(dirname "$file")/references"
279:   if [[ -d "$refs_src" ]]; then
280:     mkdir -p "$dest_dir/${slug}-references"
281:     ats_cp_r "$refs_src" "$dest_dir/${slug}-references"
282:   fi
283:   copy_scripts "$file" "$dest_dir" "$slug"
284: }
285: 
286: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
287: # Converter: antigravity
288: # Generated frontmatter: name, description, risk, source, date_added.
289: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
290: convert_antigravity() {
291:   local file="$1" slug="$3"
292:   # shellcheck disable=SC2034 # category unused but kept for consistent signature
293:   local category="$2"
294:   local dest_dir="$OUT_DIR/antigravity/$slug"
295:   local dest_file="$dest_dir/SKILL.md"
296:   local description body
297:   mkdir -p "$dest_dir"
298: 
299:   description="$(get_field_raw "description" "$file")"
300:   body="$(get_body "$file")"
301: 
302:   {
303:     printf -- '---\n'
304:     printf 'name: %s\n' "$slug"
305:     printf 'description: |\n'
306:     while IFS= read -r dline; do
307:       printf '  %s\n' "$dline"
308:     done <<< "$description"
309:     printf 'risk: low\n'
310:     printf 'source: alltheskills\n'
311:     printf "date_added: '%s'\n" "$TODAY"
312:     printf -- '---\n'
313:     printf '%s\n' "$body"
314:   } > "$dest_file"
315: 
316:   copy_references "$file" "$dest_dir"
317:   copy_scripts "$file" "$dest_dir"
318: }

329:   local dest_file="$dest_dir/SKILL.md"
330:   local description body
331:   mkdir -p "$dest_dir"
332: 
333:   description="$(get_field_raw "description" "$file")"
334:   body="$(get_body "$file")"
335: 
336:   {
337:     printf -- '---\n'
338:     printf 'name: %s\n' "$slug"
339:     printf 'description: |\n'
340:     while IFS= read -r dline; do
341:       printf '  %s\n' "$dline"
342:     done <<< "$description"
343:     printf -- '---\n'
344:     printf '%s\n' "$body"
345:   } > "$dest_file"
346: 
347:   copy_references "$file" "$dest_dir"
348:   copy_scripts "$file" "$dest_dir"

351: write_gemini_manifest() {
352:   local manifest="$OUT_DIR/gemini-cli/gemini-extension.json"
353:   mkdir -p "$OUT_DIR/gemini-cli"
354:   cat > "$manifest" <<'JSON'
355: {
356:   "name": "alltheskills",
357:   "version": "1.0.0"
358: }
359: JSON
360: }

362: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
363: # Converter: opencode
364: # name (original), description, mode=subagent, color=#6B7280
365: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
366: convert_opencode() {
367:   local file="$1" slug="$3"
368:   # shellcheck disable=SC2034 # category unused
369:   local category="$2"
370:   local dest_dir="$OUT_DIR/opencode/agents"
371:   local dest_file="$dest_dir/${slug}.md"
372:   local name description body
373:   mkdir -p "$dest_dir"
374: 
375:   name="$(get_field "name" "$file")"
376:   description="$(get_field_raw "description" "$file")"
377:   body="$(get_body "$file")"
378: 
379:   {
380:     printf -- '---\n'
381:     printf 'name: %s\n' "$name"
382:     printf 'description: |\n'
383:     while IFS= read -r dline; do
384:       printf '  %s\n' "$dline"
385:     done <<< "$description"
386:     printf 'mode: subagent\n'
387:     printf "color: '#6B7280'\n"
388:     printf -- '---\n'
389:     printf '%s\n' "$body"
390:   } > "$dest_file"
391: 
392:   # Copy references + scripts as <slug>-*/ siblings
393:   local refs_src
394:   refs_src="$(dirname "$file")/references"
395:   if [[ -d "$refs_src" ]]; then
396:     mkdir -p "$dest_dir/${slug}-references"
397:     ats_cp_r "$refs_src" "$dest_dir/${slug}-references"
398:   fi
399:   copy_scripts "$file" "$dest_dir" "$slug"
400: }
401: 
402: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
403: # Converter: cursor
404: # description/globs/alwaysApply frontmatter + inline references.
405: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
406: convert_cursor() {
407:   local file="$1" slug="$3"
408:   # shellcheck disable=SC2034 # category unused
409:   local category="$2"
410:   local dest_dir="$OUT_DIR/cursor/rules"
411:   local dest_file="$dest_dir/${slug}.mdc"
412:   local description body
413:   mkdir -p "$dest_dir"
414: 
415:   description="$(get_field_raw "description" "$file")"
416:   body="$(get_body "$file")"
417: 
418:   {
419:     printf -- '---\n'
420:     printf 'description: |\n'
421:     while IFS= read -r dline; do
422:       printf '  %s\n' "$dline"
423:     done <<< "$description"
424:     printf 'globs: ""\n'
425:     printf 'alwaysApply: false\n'
426:     printf -- '---\n'
427:     printf '%s\n' "$body"
428:     inline_references "$file"
429:   } > "$dest_file"
430:   copy_scripts "$file" "$dest_dir" "$slug"
431: }
432: 
433: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
434: # Converter: openclaw
435: # Split body by ## headers into SOUL.md / AGENTS.md + IDENTITY.md.
436: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
437: 
438: # Classify a ## header line as "soul" or "agents"
439: _classify_header() {
440:   local hdr_lower="$1"
441:   if echo "$hdr_lower" | grep -qiE '(identity|learning.*memory|communication|style|critical.rule|rules you must follow)'; then
442:     printf 'soul'
443:   else
444:     printf 'agents'
445:   fi
446: }
447: 
448: convert_openclaw() {
449:   local file="$1" slug="$3"
450:   # shellcheck disable=SC2034 # category unused
451:   local category="$2"
452:   local dest_dir="$OUT_DIR/openclaw/$slug"
453:   local name description body
454:   mkdir -p "$dest_dir"
455: 
456:   name="$(get_field "name" "$file")"
457:   description="$(get_field_raw "description" "$file")"
458:   body="$(get_body "$file")"
459: 
460:   # --- Split body into SOUL vs AGENTS sections ---
461:   local soul_tmp agents_tmp
462:   soul_tmp="$(ats_mktemp_file)"
463:   agents_tmp="$(ats_mktemp_file)"
464:   # shellcheck disable=SC2064 # we want current values of soul_tmp/agents_tmp captured now
465:   trap "rm -f '$soul_tmp' '$agents_tmp'" RETURN
466: 
467:   local current_target="agents"
468:   local current_section=""
469:   local has_headers=0
470: 
471:   while IFS= read -r line; do
472:     if [[ "$line" =~ ^##[[:space:]] ]]; then
473:       has_headers=1
474:       # Flush previous section
475:       if [[ -n "$current_section" ]]; then
476:         if [[ "$current_target" == "soul" ]]; then
477:           printf '%s' "$current_section" >> "$soul_tmp"
478:         else
479:           printf '%s' "$current_section" >> "$agents_tmp"
480:         fi
481:       fi
482:       current_section=""
483: 
484:       local hdr_lower
485:       hdr_lower="$(printf '%s' "$line" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')"
486:       current_target="$(_classify_header "$hdr_lower")"
487:     fi
488:     current_section="${current_section}${line}
489: "
490:   done <<< "$body"
491: 
492:   # Flush final section
493:   if [[ -n "$current_section" ]]; then
494:     if [[ "$current_target" == "soul" ]]; then
495:       printf '%s' "$current_section" >> "$soul_tmp"
496:     else
497:       printf '%s' "$current_section" >> "$agents_tmp"
498:     fi
499:   fi
500: 
501:   # Write SOUL.md
502:   if [[ "$has_headers" -eq 0 ]]; then
503:     # No headers: put everything in AGENTS, SOUL is a placeholder
504:     printf '# %s\n' "$name" > "$dest_dir/SOUL.md"
505:     {
506:       printf '%s\n' "$body"
507:     } > "$dest_dir/AGENTS.md"
508:   else
509:     local soul_content agents_content
510:     soul_content="$(cat "$soul_tmp")"
511:     agents_content="$(cat "$agents_tmp")"
512: 
513:     if [[ -n "$soul_content" ]]; then
514:       printf '%s\n' "$soul_content" > "$dest_dir/SOUL.md"
515:     else
516:       printf '# %s\n' "$name" > "$dest_dir/SOUL.md"
517:     fi
518: 
519:     {
520:       if [[ -n "$agents_content" ]]; then
521:         printf '%s\n' "$agents_content"
522:       fi
523:       # Footer for references
524:       local refs_src
525:       refs_src="$(dirname "$file")/references"
526:       if [[ -d "$refs_src" ]]; then
527:         printf '\n> Additional context: see references/\n'
528:       fi
529:     } > "$dest_dir/AGENTS.md"
530:   fi
531: 
532:   # Write IDENTITY.md — 3-line format with robot emoji (Slice A default)
533:   {
534:     printf '# \xf0\x9f\xa4\x96 %s\n' "$name"
535:     # description on next line, collapsed to one line
536:     printf '%s\n' "$(get_field "description" "$file")"
537:   } > "$dest_dir/IDENTITY.md"
538: 
539:   # Copy references + scripts
540:   copy_references "$file" "$dest_dir"
541:   copy_scripts "$file" "$dest_dir"
542: }
543: 
544: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
545: # Converter: qwen
546: # name, description, optional tools (mapped from allowed_tools).
547: # Body + inline references.
548: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
549: convert_qwen() {
550:   local file="$1" slug="$3"
551:   # shellcheck disable=SC2034 # category unused
552:   local category="$2"
553:   local dest_dir="$OUT_DIR/qwen/agents"
554:   local dest_file="$dest_dir/${slug}.md"
555:   local description body allowed_tools_csv
556:   mkdir -p "$dest_dir"
557: 
558:   description="$(get_field_raw "description" "$file")"
559:   body="$(get_body "$file")"
560:   # Map allowed-tools (comma-separated list from get_field; the deprecated
561:   # allowed_tools alias is honored by the parser) to qwen's tools field
562:   allowed_tools_csv="$(get_field "allowed-tools" "$file")"
563: 
564:   {
565:     printf -- '---\n'
566:     printf 'name: %s\n' "$slug"
567:     printf 'description: |\n'
568:     while IFS= read -r dline; do
569:       printf '  %s\n' "$dline"
570:     done <<< "$description"
571:     if [[ -n "$allowed_tools_csv" ]]; then
572:       printf 'tools: %s\n' "$allowed_tools_csv"
573:     fi
574:     printf -- '---\n'
575:     printf '%s\n' "$body"
576:     inline_references "$file"
577:   } > "$dest_file"
578:   copy_scripts "$file" "$dest_dir" "$slug"
579: }
580: 
581: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
582: # Converter: kimi
583: # agent.yaml + system.md with inline references.
584: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
585: convert_kimi() {
586:   local file="$1" slug="$3"
587:   # shellcheck disable=SC2034 # category unused
588:   local category="$2"
589:   local dest_dir="$OUT_DIR/kimi/$slug"
590:   local name description body
591:   mkdir -p "$dest_dir"
592: 
593:   name="$(get_field "name" "$file")"
594:   description="$(get_field "description" "$file")"
595:   body="$(get_body "$file")"
596: 
597:   cat > "$dest_dir/agent.yaml" <<YAML
598: version: 1
599: agent:
600:   name: ${slug}
601:   extend: default
602:   system_prompt_path: ./system.md
603: YAML
604: 
605:   {
606:     printf '# %s\n\n' "$name"
607:     printf '%s\n\n' "$description"
608:     printf '%s\n' "$body"
609:     inline_references "$file"
610:   } > "$dest_dir/system.md"
611:   copy_scripts "$file" "$dest_dir"
612: }
613: 
614: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
615: # Accumulator: aider
616: # All skills concatenated into one CONVENTIONS.md.
617: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
618: AIDER_TMP=''
619: WINDSURF_TMP=''
620: AIDER_COUNT=0
621: WINDSURF_COUNT=0
622: 
623: init_accumulators() {
624:   AIDER_TMP="$(ats_mktemp_file)"
625:   WINDSURF_TMP="$(ats_mktemp_file)"
626: 
627:   cat > "$AIDER_TMP" <<'HEADER'
628: # Skill Madness — Skill Conventions
629: #
630: # Generated by scripts/convert.sh — do not edit manually.
631: # Source: https://github.com/ivy00johns/Skill-Madness
632: #
633: # To activate a skill in Aider, reference it by name in your prompt, e.g.:
634: #   "Apply the backend-agent skill to refactor this service."
635: #
636: HEADER
637: 
638:   cat > "$WINDSURF_TMP" <<'HEADER'
639: # Skill Madness — Skill Rules for Windsurf
640: #
641: # Generated by scripts/convert.sh — do not edit manually.
642: #
643: HEADER
644: }
645: 
646: finalize_accumulators() {
647:   if [[ "$_DO_AIDER" == "1" || "$_DO_ALL" == "1" ]]; then
648:     mkdir -p "$OUT_DIR/aider"
649:     cp "$AIDER_TMP" "$OUT_DIR/aider/CONVENTIONS.md"
650:   fi
651:   if [[ "$_DO_WINDSURF" == "1" || "$_DO_ALL" == "1" ]]; then
652:     mkdir -p "$OUT_DIR/windsurf"
653:     cp "$WINDSURF_TMP" "$OUT_DIR/windsurf/.windsurfrules"
654:   fi
655: }
656: 
657: accumulate_aider() {
658:   local file="$1" slug="$3"
659:   # shellcheck disable=SC2034 # category unused
660:   local category="$2"
661:   local name body
662: 
663:   name="$(get_field "name" "$file")"
664:   body="$(get_body "$file")"
665: 
666:   {
667:     printf -- '\n---\n\n'
668:     printf '## %s\n\n' "$name"
669:     printf '> %s\n\n' "$(get_field "description" "$file")"
670:     printf '%s\n' "$body"
671:   } >> "$AIDER_TMP"
672: 
673:   # Warn about skipped references
674:   local refs_src
675:   refs_src="$(dirname "$file")/references"
676:   if [[ -d "$refs_src" ]]; then
677:     printf '[aider] skipped references for %s\n' "$slug" >&2
678:   fi
679:   AIDER_COUNT=$(( AIDER_COUNT + 1 ))
680: }
681: 
682: accumulate_windsurf() {
683:   local file="$1" slug="$3"
684:   # shellcheck disable=SC2034 # category unused
685:   local category="$2"
686:   local name body
687: 
688:   name="$(get_field "name" "$file")"
689:   body="$(get_body "$file")"
690: 
691:   {
692:     printf '\n'
693:     repeat_char '=' 80
694:     printf '\n## %s\n%s\n' "$name" "$(get_field "description" "$file")"
695:     repeat_char '=' 80
696:     printf '\n\n%s\n' "$body"
697:   } >> "$WINDSURF_TMP"
698: 
699:   # Warn about skipped references
700:   local refs_src
701:   refs_src="$(dirname "$file")/references"
702:   if [[ -d "$refs_src" ]]; then
703:     printf '[windsurf] skipped references for %s\n' "$slug" >&2
704:   fi
705:   WINDSURF_COUNT=$(( WINDSURF_COUNT + 1 ))
706: }
707: 
708: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
709: # process_skill <file> <tool>
710: # Dispatch to the correct converter.
711: # Returns: 0 ok, 1 skip (requires_claude_code + non-cc tool), 2 hard error.
712: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
713: process_skill() {
714:   local file="$1" tool="$2"
715:   local category slug
716: 
717:   derive_category_slug "$file"
718:   category="$SKILL_CATEGORY"
719:   slug="$SKILL_SLUG"
720: 
721:   # Check requires_claude_code for non-claude-code tools
722:   if [[ "$tool" != "claude-code" ]]; then
723:     local req_cc
724:     req_cc="$(get_field "requires_claude_code" "$file")"
725:     if [[ "$req_cc" == "true" ]]; then
726:       printf '[convert] skipping %s/%s for %s (requires_claude_code: true)\n' \
727:         "$category" "$slug" "$tool" >&2
728:       return 1
729:     fi
730:   fi
731: 
732:   case "$tool" in
733:     claude-code)  convert_claude_code  "$file" "$category" "$slug" ;;
734:     copilot)      convert_copilot      "$file" "$category" "$slug" ;;
735:     antigravity)  convert_antigravity  "$file" "$category" "$slug" ;;
736:     gemini-cli)   convert_gemini_cli   "$file" "$category" "$slug" ;;
737:     opencode)     convert_opencode     "$file" "$category" "$slug" ;;
738:     cursor)       convert_cursor       "$file" "$category" "$slug" ;;
739:     openclaw)     convert_openclaw     "$file" "$category" "$slug" ;;
740:     qwen)         convert_qwen         "$file" "$category" "$slug" ;;
741:     kimi)         convert_kimi         "$file" "$category" "$slug" ;;
742:     aider)        accumulate_aider     "$file" "$category" "$slug" ;;
743:     windsurf)     accumulate_windsurf  "$file" "$category" "$slug" ;;
744:     *) printf '[convert] ERROR: unknown tool: %s\n' "$tool" >&2; return 2 ;;
745:   esac
746: }
747: 
748: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
749: # run_tool <tool>
750: # Walk all skills for a single tool. Returns counts: processed skipped errors.
751: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
752: run_tool() {
753:   local tool="$1"
754:   local processed=0 skipped=0 errors=0
755:   local file
756: 
757:   while IFS= read -r file; do
758:     # Validate frontmatter exists
759:     if ! fm_check "$file" 2>/dev/null; then
760:       printf '[convert] ERROR: %s: malformed frontmatter — aborting conversion\n' "$file" >&2
761:       errors=$(( errors + 1 ))
762:       continue
763:     fi
764: 
765:     local name
766:     name="$(get_field "name" "$file")"
767:     if [[ -z "$name" ]]; then
768:       printf '[convert] ERROR: %s: missing required field "name"\n' "$file" >&2
769:       errors=$(( errors + 1 ))
770:       continue
771:     fi
772: 
773:     local rc=0
774:     process_skill "$file" "$tool" || rc=$?
775:     case "$rc" in
776:       0) processed=$(( processed + 1 )) ;;
777:       1) skipped=$(( skipped + 1 ))   ;;
778:       *) errors=$(( errors + 1 ))     ;;
779:     esac
780:   done < <(collect_skills)
781: 
782:   printf '%s %s %s\n' "$processed" "$skipped" "$errors"
783: }
784: 
785: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
786: # Parallel worker entry point.
787: # When ATS_INSTALL_WORKER=1, run a single tool and exit.
788: # (Used by parallel mode — parent spawns this script per tool.)
789: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
790: if [[ "${ATS_CONVERT_WORKER:-}" == "1" && -n "${ATS_CONVERT_TOOL:-}" ]]; then
791:   run_tool "${ATS_CONVERT_TOOL}"
792:   exit 0
793: fi
794: 
795: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
796: # Main
797: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
798: main() {
799:   local tool="all"
800:   local use_parallel=false
801:   local parallel_jobs
802:   parallel_jobs="$(nproc_count)"
803:   _DO_ALL=0
804:   _DO_AIDER=0
805:   _DO_WINDSURF=0
806: 
807:   while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
808:     case "$1" in
809:       --tool)     tool="${2:?'--tool requires a value'}"; shift 2 ;;
810:       --out)      OUT_DIR="${2:?'--out requires a value'}"; shift 2 ;;
811:       --parallel) use_parallel=true; shift ;;
812:       --jobs)     parallel_jobs="${2:?'--jobs requires a value'}"; shift 2 ;;
813:       --help|-h)  usage ;;
814:       *)          ats_err "Unknown option: $1"; exit 2 ;;
815:     esac
816:   done
817: 
818:   # Validate tool argument
819:   local valid=false t
820:   for t in "${ALL_TOOLS[@]}" all; do
821:     [[ "$t" == "$tool" ]] && valid=true && break
822:   done
823:   if ! $valid; then
824:     ats_err "Unknown tool '$tool'. Valid: ${ALL_TOOLS[*]} all"
825:     exit 2
826:   fi
827: 
828:   local tools_to_run=()
829:   if [[ "$tool" == "all" ]]; then
830:     tools_to_run=("${ALL_TOOLS[@]}")
831:     _DO_ALL=1
832:   else
833:     tools_to_run=("$tool")
834:     [[ "$tool" == "aider" ]]    && _DO_AIDER=1
835:     [[ "$tool" == "windsurf" ]] && _DO_WINDSURF=1
836:   fi
837: 
838:   # Initialize accumulator temp files (needed before worker dispatch).
839:   # This trap replaces the one lib/frontmatter.sh registered at source time,
840:   # so it must also clear the frontmatter parse cache.
841:   init_accumulators
842:   trap 'rm -f "$AIDER_TMP" "$WINDSURF_TMP"; fm_cache_clear' EXIT
843: 
844:   ats_header "Skill Madness — Converting skills to tool-specific formats"
845:   printf '  Repo:    %s\n' "$REPO_ROOT"
846:   printf '  Output:  %s\n' "$OUT_DIR"
847:   printf '  Tools:   %s\n' "${tools_to_run[*]}"
848:   printf '  Date:    %s\n' "$TODAY"
849:   printf '\n'
850: 
851:   local total_processed=0 total_skipped=0 total_errors=0
852:   local n_tools=${#tools_to_run[@]}
853: 
854:   if $use_parallel && [[ "${#tools_to_run[@]}" -gt 1 ]]; then
855:     # Parallel path: spawn one worker per tool.
856:     # Tools that accumulate (aider, windsurf) must run in the parent process
857:     # because they write to shared temp files. Run them serially after.
858:     local parallel_tools=()
859:     local serial_tools=()
860:     for t in "${tools_to_run[@]}"; do
861:       case "$t" in
862:         aider|windsurf) serial_tools+=("$t") ;;
863:         *)              parallel_tools+=("$t") ;;
864:       esac
865:     done
866: 
867:     local par_out_dir
868:     par_out_dir="$(ats_mktemp_dir)"
869: 
870:     ats_info "Parallel mode: ${#parallel_tools[@]} tools in parallel, ${#serial_tools[@]} serial (aider/windsurf)"
871: 
872:     if [[ ${#parallel_tools[@]} -gt 0 ]]; then
873:       # Export context for worker subshells
874:       export ATS_CONVERT_WORKER=1
875:       export OUT_DIR SKILLS_ROOT TODAY REPO_ROOT
876:       local pt
877:       # Write tool names to a temp file for xargs
878:       local tools_list
879:       tools_list="$(ats_mktemp_file)"
880:       for pt in "${parallel_tools[@]}"; do printf '%s\n' "$pt"; done > "$tools_list"
881:       # shellcheck disable=SC2016 # xargs shell uses single quotes intentionally
882:       xargs -P "$parallel_jobs" -I {} sh -c \
883:         'ATS_CONVERT_TOOL="{}" ATS_CONVERT_WORKER=1 '"$SCRIPT_DIR"'/convert.sh --tool "{}" --out "'"$OUT_DIR"'" > "'"$par_out_dir"'/{}" 2>&1' \
884:         < "$tools_list"
885:       rm -f "$tools_list"
886:       unset ATS_CONVERT_WORKER
887: 
888:       for pt in "${parallel_tools[@]}"; do
889:         [[ -f "$par_out_dir/$pt" ]] && cat "$par_out_dir/$pt"
890:       done
891:       rm -rf "$par_out_dir"
892:     fi
893: 
894:     for t in "${serial_tools[@]}"; do
895:       ats_header "Converting: $t"
896:       local counts
897:       counts="$(run_tool "$t")"
898:       local p s e
899:       p="$(printf '%s' "$counts" | awk '{print $1}')"
900:       s="$(printf '%s' "$counts" | awk '{print $2}')"
901:       e="$(printf '%s' "$counts" | awk '{print $3}')"
902:       total_processed=$(( total_processed + p ))
903:       total_skipped=$(( total_skipped + s ))
904:       total_errors=$(( total_errors + e ))
905:       ats_ok "$t: $p converted, $s skipped, $e errors"
906:     done
907:   else
908:     # Sequential path
909:     local i=0
910:     for t in "${tools_to_run[@]}"; do
911:       (( i++ )) || true
912:       progress_bar "$i" "$n_tools"
913:       printf '\n'
914:       ats_header "Converting: $t ($i/$n_tools)"
915: 
916:       local counts
917:       counts="$(run_tool "$t")"
918:       local p s e
919:       p="$(printf '%s' "$counts" | awk '{print $1}')"
920:       s="$(printf '%s' "$counts" | awk '{print $2}')"
921:       e="$(printf '%s' "$counts" | awk '{print $3}')"
922:       total_processed=$(( total_processed + p ))
923:       total_skipped=$(( total_skipped + s ))
924:       total_errors=$(( total_errors + e ))
925:       ats_ok "$t: $p converted, $s skipped, $e errors"
926: 
927:       # Write gemini manifest after converting gemini-cli
928:       if [[ "$t" == "gemini-cli" ]]; then
929:         write_gemini_manifest
930:         ats_info "Wrote integrations/gemini-cli/gemini-extension.json"
931:       fi
932:     done
933:   fi
934: 
935:   # Finalize accumulated single-file outputs
936:   finalize_accumulators
937: 
938:   # Hooks layer emission (contract: contracts/hooks/hooks-layer.md §4).
939:   # Only Claude Code has a native lifecycle-hook system; emit its hooks tree +
940:   # hooks.json. Every other tool is logged as unsupported (do not fabricate
941:   # hook support for tools that lack a hook runtime).
942:   for t in "${tools_to_run[@]}"; do
943:     if [[ "$t" == "claude-code" ]]; then
944:       convert_hooks_claude_code
945:     else
946:       ats_info "hooks: unsupported for $t"
947:     fi
948:   done
949: 
950:   if [[ "$tool" == "all" || "$tool" == "aider" ]]; then
951:     ats_ok "Wrote integrations/aider/CONVENTIONS.md"
952:   fi
953:   if [[ "$tool" == "all" || "$tool" == "windsurf" ]]; then
954:     ats_ok "Wrote integrations/windsurf/.windsurfrules"
955:   fi
956: 
957:   # Write gemini manifest when running all (sequential path)
958:   if [[ "$tool" == "all" ]] && ! $use_parallel; then
959:     write_gemini_manifest
960:   fi
961: 
962:   # Stderr summary (contract requirement)
963:   printf '[convert] processed %d skills across %d tools (%d skipped, %d errors)\n' \
964:     "$total_processed" "$n_tools" "$total_skipped" "$total_errors" >&2
965: 
966:   if (( total_errors > 0 )); then
967:     exit 1
968:   fi
969: }
970: 
971: main "$@"

=== scripts/install.sh ===
1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
2: #
3: # install.sh — Install Skill Madness into local agentic tools.
4: #
5: # Reads converted artifacts from integrations/ and copies them to the
6: # appropriate config directories for each tool. Run scripts/convert.sh first.
7: #
8: # Usage:
9: #   scripts/install.sh [OPTIONS] [TOOL ...]
10: #
11: # Options:
12: #   --tool NAME          Install for a single tool (repeatable)
13: #   --all                Install for all 11 tools
14: #   --detected           Install only for detected tools (default in non-interactive)
15: #   --interactive        Force the TUI (default if stdin is a TTY)
16: #   --no-interactive     Skip the TUI (default if stdin is not a TTY)
17: #   --parallel           Run installations concurrently
18: #   --jobs N             Worker count for --parallel (default: nproc / sysctl)
19: #   --dry-run            Print what would be copied; do not write
20: #   --wire-hooks         OPT-IN: merge the converted ats-hooks into
21: #                        ~/.claude/settings.json (backs up first; idempotent).
22: #                        Default is no-change: only the merge snippet is printed.
23: #   --help               Print this and exit
24: #
25: # Exit codes: 0 success, 1 install error, 2 argument/preflight error
26: #
27: # Portions of this script (the per-tool detection helpers and the interactive
28: # selection UI) are adapted from msitarzewski/agency-agents (MIT). See
29: # ACKNOWLEDGMENTS.md at the repo root.
30: 
31: set -euo pipefail
32: 
33: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
34: LIB_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/lib"
35: 
36: # shellcheck source=scripts/lib/term.sh
37: . "$LIB_DIR/term.sh"
38: # shellcheck source=scripts/lib/platform.sh
39: . "$LIB_DIR/platform.sh"
40: 
41: REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/.." && pwd)"
42: INTEGRATIONS="$REPO_ROOT/integrations"
43: 
44: ALL_TOOLS=(claude-code copilot antigravity gemini-cli opencode cursor openclaw qwen kimi aider windsurf)
45: 
46: DRY_RUN=false
47: 
48: # OPT-IN: merge the converted ats-hooks (hooks.json) into ~/.claude/settings.json
49: # during a claude-code install. Default OFF (no-change) — the installer only ever
50: # prints the merge snippet unless the user asks. Set by --wire-hooks; also honored
51: # via ATS_WIRE_HOOKS=1 so parallel workers (which bypass main's arg parsing)
52: # inherit the choice. Read here, before the worker entry point below.
53: WIRE_HOOKS=false
54: [[ "${ATS_WIRE_HOOKS:-}" == "1" ]] && WIRE_HOOKS=true
55: 
56: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
57: # Usage
58: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
59: usage() {
60:   sed -n '3,23p' "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'
61:   exit 0
62: }
63: 
64: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
65: # Dry-run aware copy helpers
66: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
67: 
68: # install_file <src> <dst>
69: # Copy src to dst, respecting DRY_RUN. Handles symlink replacement and
70: # identical-file optimization.
71: install_file() {
72:   local src="$1" dst="$2"
73: 
74:   if $DRY_RUN; then
75:     printf '  [dry-run] would copy: %s -> %s\n' "$src" "$dst"
76:     return 0
77:   fi
78: 
79:   mkdir -p "$(dirname "$dst")"
80: 
81:   # Destination is a symlink: replace with regular file
82:   if [[ -L "$dst" ]]; then
83:     ats_warn "[install] replaced symlink at $dst"
84:     rm "$dst"
85:   fi
86: 
87:   # If files are identical, skip silently (or note at --verbose)
88:   if [[ -f "$dst" ]] && cmp -s "$src" "$dst"; then
89:     return 0
90:   fi
91: 
92:   cp "$src" "$dst"
93:   printf '[install] updated %s\n' "$dst"
94: }
95: 
96: # install_dir <src_dir> <dst_dir>
97: # Recursively install all files from src_dir into dst_dir.
98: install_dir() {
99:   local src_dir="$1" dst_dir="$2"
100:   local f rel_path dst_file
101: 
102:   while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
103:     rel_path="${f#"${src_dir}"/}"
104:     dst_file="$dst_dir/$rel_path"
105:     install_file "$f" "$dst_file"
106:   done < <(find "$src_dir" -type f -print0 | sort -z)
107: }
108: 
109: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
110: # Preflight checks
111: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
112: check_integrations() {
113:   if [[ ! -d "$INTEGRATIONS" ]]; then
114:     ats_err "integrations/ not found. Run scripts/convert.sh first."
115:     exit 2
116:   fi
117: }
118: 
119: check_tool_integration() {
120:   local tool="$1"
121:   if [[ ! -d "$INTEGRATIONS/$tool" ]]; then
122:     ats_err "integrations/$tool/ not found. Run: scripts/convert.sh --tool $tool"
123:     return 1
124:   fi
125:   return 0
126: }
127: 
128: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
129: # Tool detection
130: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
131: detect_claude_code()  { [[ -d "${HOME}/.claude" ]]; }
132: detect_copilot()      { command -v code >/dev/null 2>&1 || [[ -d "${HOME}/.github" || -d "${HOME}/.copilot" ]]; }
133: detect_antigravity()  { [[ -d "${HOME}/.gemini/antigravity/skills" ]]; }
134: detect_gemini_cli()   { command -v gemini >/dev/null 2>&1 || [[ -d "${HOME}/.gemini" ]]; }
135: detect_opencode()     { command -v opencode >/dev/null 2>&1 || [[ -d "$PWD/.opencode" ]]; }
136: detect_cursor()       { command -v cursor >/dev/null 2>&1 || [[ -d "${HOME}/.cursor" ]]; }
137: detect_openclaw()     { command -v openclaw >/dev/null 2>&1 || [[ -d "${HOME}/.openclaw" ]]; }
138: detect_qwen()         { command -v qwen >/dev/null 2>&1; }
139: detect_kimi()         { command -v kimi >/dev/null 2>&1 || [[ -d "${HOME}/.config/kimi" ]]; }
140: detect_aider()        { command -v aider >/dev/null 2>&1; }
141: detect_windsurf()     { command -v windsurf >/dev/null 2>&1 || [[ -d "${HOME}/.codeium/windsurf" ]]; }
142: 
143: is_detected() {
144:   case "$1" in
145:     claude-code)  detect_claude_code  ;;
146:     copilot)      detect_copilot      ;;
147:     antigravity)  detect_antigravity  ;;
148:     gemini-cli)   detect_gemini_cli   ;;
149:     opencode)     detect_opencode     ;;
150:     cursor)       detect_cursor       ;;
151:     openclaw)     detect_openclaw     ;;
152:     qwen)         detect_qwen         ;;
153:     kimi)         detect_kimi         ;;
154:     aider)        detect_aider        ;;
155:     windsurf)     detect_windsurf     ;;
156:     *)            return 1            ;;
157:   esac
158: }
159: 
160: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
161: # Tool labels for TUI
162: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
163: tool_label() {
164:   case "$1" in
165:     claude-code)  printf '%-14s  %s' 'Claude Code'  '(~/.claude/skills/)'          ;;
166:     copilot)      printf '%-14s  %s' 'Copilot'      '(~/.github + ~/.copilot)'     ;;
167:     antigravity)  printf '%-14s  %s' 'Antigravity'  '(~/.gemini/antigravity)'      ;;
168:     gemini-cli)   printf '%-14s  %s' 'Gemini CLI'   '(gemini extension)'           ;;
169:     opencode)     printf '%-14s  %s' 'OpenCode'     '(.opencode/agents)'           ;;
170:     cursor)       printf '%-14s  %s' 'Cursor'       '(.cursor/rules)'              ;;
171:     openclaw)     printf '%-14s  %s' 'OpenClaw'     '(~/.openclaw/alltheskills)'   ;;
172:     qwen)         printf '%-14s  %s' 'Qwen Code'    '(.qwen/agents)'               ;;
173:     kimi)         printf '%-14s  %s' 'Kimi Code'    '(~/.config/kimi/agents)'      ;;
174:     aider)        printf '%-14s  %s' 'Aider'        '(CONVENTIONS.md)'            ;;
175:     windsurf)     printf '%-14s  %s' 'Windsurf'     '(.windsurfrules)'             ;;
176:     *)            printf '%s' "$1" ;;
177:   esac
178: }
179: 
180: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
181: # Interactive TUI selector
182: # Sets SELECTED_TOOLS array on exit.
183: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
184: interactive_select() {
185:   # Bash 3.2-safe: use indexed arrays with numeric indices
186:   local i t num
187:   local n_tools=${#ALL_TOOLS[@]}
188:   local selected=()
189:   local detected_map=()
190: 
191:   for (( i=0; i<n_tools; i++ )); do
192:     t="${ALL_TOOLS[$i]}"
193:     if is_detected "$t" 2>/dev/null; then
194:       selected+=(1); detected_map+=(1)
195:     else
196:       selected+=(0); detected_map+=(0)
197:     fi
198:   done
199: 
200:   while true; do
201:     printf '\n'
202:     box_top
203:     box_row "${C_BOLD}  Skill Madness -- Skill Installer${C_RESET}"
204:     box_bot
205:     printf '\n'
206:     printf '  %sSystem scan:  [*] = detected on this machine%s\n' "${C_DIM}" "${C_RESET}"
207:     printf '\n'
208: 
209:     for (( i=0; i<n_tools; i++ )); do
210:       t="${ALL_TOOLS[$i]}"
211:       num=$(( i + 1 ))
212:       local label dot chk
213:       label="$(tool_label "$t")"
214:       if [[ "${detected_map[$i]}" == "1" ]]; then
215:         dot="${C_GREEN}[*]${C_RESET}"
216:       else
217:         dot="${C_DIM}[ ]${C_RESET}"
218:       fi
219:       if [[ "${selected[$i]}" == "1" ]]; then
220:         chk="${C_GREEN}[x]${C_RESET}"
221:       else
222:         chk="${C_DIM}[ ]${C_RESET}"
223:       fi
224:       printf '  %s  %s)  %s  %s\n' "$chk" "$num" "$dot" "$label"
225:     done
226: 
227:     printf '\n'
228:     printf '  ------------------------------------------------\n'
229:     printf '  %s[1-%s]%s toggle   %s[a]%s all   %s[n]%s none   %s[d]%s detected\n' \
230:       "${C_CYAN}" "$n_tools" "${C_RESET}" \
231:       "${C_CYAN}" "${C_RESET}" \
232:       "${C_CYAN}" "${C_RESET}" \
233:       "${C_CYAN}" "${C_RESET}"
234:     printf '  %s[Enter]%s install   %s[q]%s quit\n' \
235:       "${C_GREEN}" "${C_RESET}" "${C_RED}" "${C_RESET}"
236:     printf '\n'
237:     printf '  >> '
238: 
239:     local input=''
240:     read -r input </dev/tty || true
241: 
242:     case "$input" in
243:       q|Q)
244:         printf '\n'; ats_ok 'Aborted.'; exit 0 ;;
245:       a|A)
246:         # shellcheck disable=SC2004 # arithmetic array indexing: $i is conventional bash style
247:         for (( i=0; i<n_tools; i++ )); do selected[$i]=1; done ;;
248:       n|N)
249:         # shellcheck disable=SC2004
250:         for (( i=0; i<n_tools; i++ )); do selected[$i]=0; done ;;
251:       d|D)
252:         # shellcheck disable=SC2004
253:         for (( i=0; i<n_tools; i++ )); do selected[$i]="${detected_map[$i]}"; done ;;
254:       '')
255:         local any=false s
256:         for s in "${selected[@]}"; do [[ "$s" == "1" ]] && any=true && break; done
257:         if $any; then
258:           break
259:         else
260:           printf '  %sNothing selected. Pick a tool or press q to quit.%s\n' "${C_YELLOW}" "${C_RESET}"
261:         fi ;;
262:       *)
263:         local toggled=false
264:         # Allow space-separated or single number input
265:         for num in $input; do
266:           if [[ "$num" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
267:             local idx=$(( num - 1 ))
268:             if (( idx >= 0 && idx < n_tools )); then
269:               # shellcheck disable=SC2004 # arithmetic array indexing: $idx is conventional
270:               if [[ "${selected[$idx]}" == "1" ]]; then
271:                 selected[$idx]=0
272:               else
273:                 selected[$idx]=1
274:               fi
275:               toggled=true
276:             fi
277:           fi
278:         done
279:         if ! $toggled; then
280:           printf '  %sInvalid input. Enter 1-%s, a/n/d, or q.%s\n' \
281:             "${C_RED}" "$n_tools" "${C_RESET}"
282:         fi ;;
283:     esac
284: 
285:     # Redraw: move cursor up to clear the UI
286:     local lines=$(( n_tools + 13 ))
287:     local l
288:     for (( l=0; l<lines; l++ )); do printf '\033[1A\033[2K'; done
289:   done
290: 
291:   # Build SELECTED_TOOLS from the selected array
292:   SELECTED_TOOLS=()
293:   for (( i=0; i<n_tools; i++ )); do
294:     [[ "${selected[$i]}" == "1" ]] && SELECTED_TOOLS+=("${ALL_TOOLS[$i]}")
295:   done
296: }
297: 
298: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
299: # Installers — one per tool
300: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
301: 
302: install_claude_code() {
303:   local src="$INTEGRATIONS/claude-code"
304:   local base_dest="${HOME}/.claude/skills"
305:   local count=0 skill_dir skill_name dest_dir
306: 
307:   check_tool_integration "claude-code" || return 1
308: 
309:   # Walk category/slug dirs under integrations/claude-code/
310:   while IFS= read -r -d '' skill_dir; do
311:     skill_name="$(basename "$skill_dir")"
312:     local category
313:     category="$(basename "$(dirname "$skill_dir")")"
314: 
315:     # The hooks layer lives at integrations/claude-code/hooks/ and is installed
316:     # separately into ~/.claude/ats-hooks/ by install_hooks_claude_code — it is
317:     # not a skill category, so skip its depth-2 dirs here.
318:     if [[ "$category" == "hooks" ]]; then
319:       continue
320:     fi
321: 
322:     dest_dir="$base_dest/$category/$skill_name"
323: 
324:     # Skip dirs managed by /sync-skills (symlinks at the skill level)
325:     if [[ -L "$dest_dir" ]]; then
326:       printf '[install] skipped %s (managed by /sync-skills)\n' "$skill_name" >&2
327:       continue
328:     fi
329: 
330:     if $DRY_RUN; then
331:       printf '  [dry-run] would install: %s -> %s\n' "$skill_dir" "$dest_dir"
332:       count=$(( count + 1 ))
333:       continue
334:     fi
335: 
336:     mkdir -p "$dest_dir"
337:     install_dir "$skill_dir" "$dest_dir"
338:     count=$(( count + 1 ))
339:   done < <(find "$src" -mindepth 2 -maxdepth 2 -type d -print0 | sort -z)
340: 
341:   ats_ok "Claude Code: $count skills -> $base_dest"
342: 
343:   install_hooks_claude_code
344: }
345: 
346: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
347: # install_hooks_claude_code
348: # Copy the converted hooks tree to a NAMESPACED target (~/.claude/ats-hooks/),
349: # never the user's own hook dir, and place hooks.json there too. Does NOT
350: # auto-merge the user's ~/.claude/settings.json — instead prints the exact
351: # merge snippet + a one-line instruction. Non-destructive is mandatory.
352: # (Contract: contracts/hooks/hooks-layer.md §4.)
353: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
354: install_hooks_claude_code() {
355:   local hooks_src="$INTEGRATIONS/claude-code/hooks"
356:   local hooks_json="$INTEGRATIONS/claude-code/hooks.json"
357:   local dest="${HOME}/.claude/ats-hooks"
358: 
359:   # No hooks artifacts → nothing to do (convert.sh may not have emitted them).
360:   if [[ ! -d "$hooks_src" ]]; then
361:     return 0
362:   fi
363: 
364:   if $DRY_RUN; then
365:     printf '  [dry-run] would install hooks: %s -> %s\n' "$hooks_src" "$dest"
366:     [[ -f "$hooks_json" ]] && printf '  [dry-run] would copy: %s -> %s/hooks.json\n' "$hooks_json" "$dest"
367:     if $WIRE_HOOKS; then
368:       printf '  [dry-run] would merge hooks.json into %s/.claude/settings.json (backup first)\n' "${HOME}"
369:     fi
370:     ats_ok "Claude Code hooks: (dry-run) -> $dest"
371:     return 0
372:   fi
373: 
374:   mkdir -p "$dest"
375:   install_dir "$hooks_src" "$dest"
376:   if [[ -f "$hooks_json" ]]; then
377:     install_file "$hooks_json" "$dest/hooks.json"
378:   fi
379: 
380:   # Preserve executable bits (install_file uses cp which keeps mode, but the
381:   # source under integrations/ may not be +x if convert ran on a fresh clone).
382:   [[ -f "$dest/run-with-flags.sh" ]] && chmod +x "$dest/run-with-flags.sh"
383:   if [[ -d "$dest/scripts" ]]; then
384:     find "$dest/scripts" -type f \( -name '*.sh' -o -name '*.py' \) -exec chmod +x {} +
385:   fi
386:   [[ -f "$dest/lib/hook-flags.sh" ]] && chmod +x "$dest/lib/hook-flags.sh"
387: 
388:   ats_ok "Claude Code hooks: installed -> $dest"
389: 
390:   # Wiring hooks.json into ~/.claude/settings.json is OPT-IN; default is
391:   # NO-CHANGE. With --wire-hooks (or ATS_WIRE_HOOKS=1) we merge non-destructively;
392:   # on an interactive TTY we prompt (default no); otherwise we print the
393:   # copy-paste snippet, preserving the original non-destructive behavior.
394:   if [[ ! -f "$dest/hooks.json" ]]; then
395:     return 0
396:   fi
397:   if $WIRE_HOOKS; then
398:     merge_hooks_into_settings "$dest/hooks.json"
399:   elif [[ -t 0 && "${ATS_INSTALL_WORKER:-}" != "1" ]]; then
400:     printf '  Merge ats-hooks into ~/.claude/settings.json now? [y/N] '
401:     local reply=''
402:     read -r reply </dev/tty || true
403:     case "$reply" in
404:       y|Y|yes|YES) merge_hooks_into_settings "$dest/hooks.json" ;;
405:       *)           print_hooks_merge_snippet "$dest" ;;
406:     esac
407:   else
408:     print_hooks_merge_snippet "$dest"
409:   fi
410: }
411: 
412: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
413: # print_hooks_merge_snippet <ats-hooks-dir>
414: # The original non-destructive path: tell the user hooks are NOT auto-merged and
415: # print the hooks.json indented for a hand copy-paste into settings.json.
416: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
417: print_hooks_merge_snippet() {
418:   local dest="$1"
419:   ats_info "Claude Code hooks are NOT auto-merged into ~/.claude/settings.json."
420:   ats_info "To enable, re-run with --wire-hooks, or merge $dest/hooks.json into the \"hooks\" key of ~/.claude/settings.json:"
421:   if [[ -f "$dest/hooks.json" ]]; then
422:     sed 's/^/    /' "$dest/hooks.json"
423:   fi
424: }
425: 
426: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
427: # merge_hooks_into_settings <hooks-json>
428: # OPT-IN, non-destructive merge of the converted hooks.json into
429: # ~/.claude/settings.json. Preserves every existing setting, backs the file up
430: # before writing, and is idempotent — a hook whose command is already present is
431: # never duplicated. Never touches settings.json when there is nothing to add.
432: # All JSON handling is in python3 (stdlib) so escaping/merge is done in one place.
433: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
434: merge_hooks_into_settings() {
435:   local hooks_json="$1"
436:   local settings="${HOME}/.claude/settings.json"
437: 
438:   if [[ ! -f "$hooks_json" ]]; then
439:     ats_warn "no hooks.json at $hooks_json; nothing to wire"
440:     return 0
441:   fi
442: 
443:   local out rc=0
444:   out="$(
445:     ATS_SETTINGS="$settings" ATS_HOOKS_JSON="$hooks_json" python3 - <<'PYEOF'
446: import json, os, shutil, sys
447: from datetime import datetime, timezone
448: 
449: settings_path = os.environ["ATS_SETTINGS"]
450: hooks_path = os.environ["ATS_HOOKS_JSON"]
451: 
452: with open(hooks_path) as f:
453:     incoming = json.load(f).get("hooks", {})
454: 
455: if os.path.isfile(settings_path):
456:     try:
457:         with open(settings_path) as f:
458:             settings = json.load(f)
459:     except Exception as e:
460:         sys.stderr.write("[wire-hooks] ERROR ~/.claude/settings.json is not valid JSON (%s); refusing to touch it\n" % e)
461:         sys.exit(1)
462: else:
463:     settings = {}
464: 
465: if not isinstance(settings, dict):
466:     sys.stderr.write("[wire-hooks] ERROR settings.json top-level is not a JSON object; refusing to touch it\n")
467:     sys.exit(1)
468: 
469: hooks = settings.setdefault("hooks", {})
470: if not isinstance(hooks, dict):
471:     sys.stderr.write("[wire-hooks] ERROR settings.json 'hooks' is not an object; refusing to touch it\n")
472:     sys.exit(1)
473: 
474: def commands(entry):
475:     if not isinstance(entry, dict):
476:         return ()
477:     return tuple(sorted(
478:         h.get("command", "") for h in entry.get("hooks", []) if isinstance(h, dict)
479:     ))
480: 
481: added = 0
482: skipped = 0
483: for event, entries in incoming.items():
484:     bucket = hooks.setdefault(event, [])
485:     if not isinstance(bucket, list):
486:         sys.stderr.write("[wire-hooks] WARN settings hooks['%s'] is not a list; leaving it untouched\n" % event)
487:         continue
488:     existing = set()
489:     for e in bucket:
490:         existing.update(commands(e))
491:     for entry in entries:
492:         cmds = commands(entry)
493:         # Already wired iff every command in this entry is already present.
494:         if cmds and all(c in existing for c in cmds):
495:             skipped += 1
496:             continue
497:         bucket.append(entry)
498:         existing.update(cmds)
499:         added += 1
500: 
501: if added == 0:
502:     print("added=0 skipped=%d" % skipped)
503:     sys.exit(0)
504: 
505: # Back up the existing file before the first mutating write.
506: os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(settings_path), exist_ok=True)
507: if os.path.isfile(settings_path):
508:     ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M%S")
509:     backup = "%s.bak-%s" % (settings_path, ts)
510:     shutil.copyfile(settings_path, backup)
511:     sys.stderr.write("[wire-hooks] backed up settings -> %s\n" % backup)
512: 
513: with open(settings_path, "w") as f:
514:     json.dump(settings, f, indent=2)
515:     f.write("\n")
516: print("added=%d skipped=%d" % (added, skipped))
517: PYEOF
518:   )" || rc=$?
519: 
520:   if [[ "$rc" -ne 0 ]]; then
521:     ats_err "hook wiring skipped: could not safely merge into $settings"
522:     return 1
523:   fi
524: 
525:   case "$out" in
526:     added=0\ *) ats_ok "ats-hooks already wired into $settings (no change)" ;;
527:     *)          ats_ok "ats-hooks wired into $settings ($out)" ;;
528:   esac
529: }
530: 
531: install_copilot() {
532:   local src="$INTEGRATIONS/copilot"
533:   local dest_github="${HOME}/.github/agents"
534:   local dest_copilot="${HOME}/.copilot/agents"
535:   local count=0 f
536: 
537:   check_tool_integration "copilot" || return 1
538: 
539:   if $DRY_RUN; then
540:     while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
541:       local base; base="$(basename "$f")"
542:       printf '  [dry-run] would copy: %s -> %s\n' "$f" "$dest_github/$base"
543:       printf '  [dry-run] would copy: %s -> %s\n' "$f" "$dest_copilot/$base"
544:       count=$(( count + 1 ))
545:     done < <(find "$src" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f -print0 | sort -z)
546:     ats_ok "Copilot: $count skills (dry-run)"
547:     return 0
548:   fi
549: 
550:   mkdir -p "$dest_github" "$dest_copilot"
551: 
552:   while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
553:     local base; base="$(basename "$f")"
554:     install_file "$f" "$dest_github/$base"
555:     install_file "$f" "$dest_copilot/$base"
556:     count=$(( count + 1 ))
557:   done < <(find "$src" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f -print0 | sort -z)
558: 
559:   # Also copy reference dirs
560:   while IFS= read -r -d '' ref_dir; do
561:     local rname; rname="$(basename "$ref_dir")"
562:     install_dir "$ref_dir" "$dest_github/$rname"
563:     install_dir "$ref_dir" "$dest_copilot/$rname"
564:   done < <(find "$src" -maxdepth 1 -type d -name '*-references' -print0 | sort -z)
565: 
566:   ats_ok "Copilot: $count skills -> $dest_github and $dest_copilot"
567: }

591: install_gemini_cli() {
592:   local src="$INTEGRATIONS/gemini-cli"
593:   local dest="${HOME}/.gemini/extensions/alltheskills"
594:   local manifest="$src/gemini-extension.json"
595:   local skills_dir="$src/skills"
596:   local count=0 skill_dir skill_name
597: 
598:   check_tool_integration "gemini-cli" || return 1
599: 
600:   if [[ ! -f "$manifest" ]]; then
601:     ats_err "integrations/gemini-cli/gemini-extension.json missing. Run convert.sh --tool gemini-cli first."
602:     return 1
603:   fi
604: 
605:   if $DRY_RUN; then
606:     printf '  [dry-run] would copy: %s -> %s/gemini-extension.json\n' "$manifest" "$dest"
607:     while IFS= read -r -d '' skill_dir; do
608:       skill_name="$(basename "$skill_dir")"
609:       printf '  [dry-run] would install: %s -> %s/skills/%s\n' "$skill_dir" "$dest" "$skill_name"
610:       count=$(( count + 1 ))
611:     done < <(find "$skills_dir" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d -print0 2>/dev/null | sort -z)
612:     ats_ok "Gemini CLI: $count skills (dry-run)"
613:     return 0
614:   fi
615: 
616:   mkdir -p "$dest/skills"
617:   install_file "$manifest" "$dest/gemini-extension.json"
618: 
619:   while IFS= read -r -d '' skill_dir; do
620:     skill_name="$(basename "$skill_dir")"
621:     mkdir -p "$dest/skills/$skill_name"
622:     install_dir "$skill_dir" "$dest/skills/$skill_name"
623:     count=$(( count + 1 ))
624:   done < <(find "$skills_dir" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d -print0 2>/dev/null | sort -z)
625: 
626:   ats_ok "Gemini CLI: $count skills -> $dest"
627: }

685: install_openclaw() {
686:   local src="$INTEGRATIONS/openclaw"
687:   local dest="${HOME}/.openclaw/alltheskills"
688:   local count=0 skill_dir skill_name
689: 
690:   check_tool_integration "openclaw" || return 1
691: 
692:   while IFS= read -r -d '' skill_dir; do
693:     skill_name="$(basename "$skill_dir")"
694:     if [[ ! -f "$skill_dir/SOUL.md" || ! -f "$skill_dir/AGENTS.md" || ! -f "$skill_dir/IDENTITY.md" ]]; then
695:       continue
696:     fi
697:     if $DRY_RUN; then
698:       printf '  [dry-run] would install: %s -> %s/%s/{SOUL,AGENTS,IDENTITY}.md\n' \
699:         "$skill_dir" "$dest" "$skill_name"
700:       count=$(( count + 1 ))
701:       continue
702:     fi
703:     mkdir -p "$dest/$skill_name"
704:     install_dir "$skill_dir" "$dest/$skill_name"
705:     count=$(( count + 1 ))
706:   done < <(find "$src" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d -print0 | sort -z)
707: 
708:   ats_ok "OpenClaw: $count skills -> $dest"
709:   if command -v openclaw >/dev/null 2>&1 && ! $DRY_RUN; then
710:     ats_warn "OpenClaw: run 'openclaw gateway restart' to activate new skills"
711:   fi
712: }
